2026-11-28T01:06:24.3711589Z SOURCE_SHA="$GITHUB_SHA"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3711807Z SHORT_SHA="${SOURCE_SHA::7}"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3712024Z PUBLISH_MODE="none"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3712223Z PR_IMAGE_TAG=""
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3712407Z PUSH_OUTPUT="false"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3712863Z 
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3713114Z # workflow_call path (called from integration.yml). The planner only
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3713489Z # invokes us when a build is actually needed (no pin/cache match), so
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3713850Z # we always publish. The publish mode follows the caller's event:
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3714221Z # pull_request -> pr-scoped image (consumed by the calling Integration
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3714586Z # run via digest output, not by tag polling); main/schedule/dispatch
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3714978Z # -> pin-refresh image (operator opens a reviewed image-pin.json PR
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3715271Z # afterwards).
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3715480Z if [[ -n "$EXPECTED_FINGERPRINT" ]]; then
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3715714Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="true"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3716159Z   if [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "pull_request" && "$PR_HEAD_REPO" == "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" ]]; then
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3716637Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pr"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3716897Z     PR_IMAGE_TAG="${IMAGE_NAME}:pr-${PR_NUMBER}-${SHORT_SHA}"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3717232Z   elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "push" && "$REF" == "refs/heads/main" ]]; then
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3717520Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3717815Z   elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "schedule" || "$EVENT_NAME" == "workflow_dispatch" ]]; then
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3718125Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3718307Z   else
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3718545Z     # Untrusted PR (fork) reaching workflow_call should have been
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3718867Z     # blocked at the planner; defend in depth here too.
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3719296Z     echo "::error::workflow_call invoked from unsupported event ($EVENT_NAME, head_repo=$PR_HEAD_REPO)." >&2
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3719667Z     exit 1
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3719833Z   fi
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3720091Z # Standalone path: push to main, weekly schedule, or manual dispatch.
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3720465Z # These refresh the reviewed image-pin.json digest. The pull_request
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3720838Z # trigger no longer exists on this workflow; PR builds reach us only
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3721155Z # through the workflow_call branch above.
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3721463Z elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "push" && "$REF" == "refs/heads/main" ]]; then
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3721757Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="true"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3721961Z   PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3722172Z elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "schedule" ]]; then
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3722402Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="true"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3722802Z   PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3723030Z elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "workflow_dispatch" ]]; then
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3723300Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="${DISPATCH_PUSH}"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3723553Z   if [[ "$DISPATCH_PUSH" == "true" ]]; then
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3723790Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3723980Z   fi
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3724131Z fi
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3724285Z {
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3724454Z   echo "source_sha=${SOURCE_SHA}"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3724682Z   echo "short_sha=${SHORT_SHA}"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3724933Z   echo "cache_key=ijt-browser-ci-${SOURCE_SHA}"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3725185Z   echo "push=${PUSH_OUTPUT}"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3725571Z   echo "publish_mode=${PUBLISH_MODE}"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3725812Z   echo "pr_image_tag=${PR_IMAGE_TAG}"
2026-11-28T01:06:24.3726038Z } >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
